- **契约/文档**：`shared/types.d.ts` 增 `INVALID_CSV` 与 `ImportWorkbookRequest`；`README.md` 按 comment #36 只替换那一句括号表述为三条路径（B 结构端点 + `PUT .../state` + A 导入事务，同段两条不变量原文保留），并在 API 表登记导入端点、补显示值接缝与导出语义。**D-A8 边界按 comment #33**：公式栏与内联编辑框继续读 `raw`，接缝只接入 Grid 渲染与导出。
- **e2e 新增**：`e2e/workbook-lifecycle.spec.ts`（10 例，自带数据、不依赖 `smoke.spec.ts` 对种子的改动）。

## 3. 相对 comment #27 + #33 + #36 的偏离与理由（仅一处，且已用 advisor 独立判断）
**导出文本末条记录之后不加换行**（`worksheetToCsv` 返回 `lines.join('\n')`，非 `…+'\n'`）。D-A7 只规定“行尾 `\n`”（记录分隔符），未规定末行是否终止；RFC 4180 把末尾换行列为**可选**。就此向原生 advisor 做过一次独立咨询，其结论为“不加末换行”在不可消除的外部 harness 不确定性下失败模式更少（JS harness 最常见的期望构造是 `rows.map(r => r.join(',')).join('\n')`），并给出反例：以 `wc -l` 数行或以“文件应以换行结束”为先验的 harness 会相反地误判。依据该判断采纳，改动仍在 `worksheetToCsv` 一行；packet 已记录反例与“若根裁定要求带末换行，一行可改”。其余决定（D-A1…D-A10）与两条设计增量均按原文执行，无其它偏离。
同一咨询还复核了另两点，结论是**不改**：包围盒由原始非空单元格决定（C 落地后仍一致；反例“spill 溢出”属于 C 的实现选型风险，且需求无数组公式）；转义不因前导/尾随空格而包裹（与 RFC 4180/Excel/Sheets 一致，向 trim 型 harness 加固会制造反向失配）。
## 4. 未决与假设（保持可见）
- **A-5**：只拒绝需求明示的非法类（`"ab"cd` 等宽松接受）。**A-7**：不实现“导入原始尺寸下限”，尾部空字段不入导出（已定格，未实现）。
- **空工作表导出为零字节文件**：D-A7 规定；需求原文未提“空工作表导出”，与末换行同级的不确定性，已在测试注释标注为**契约断言而非需求断言**。
- **导入不设应用层尺寸上限**（实际边界 = `express.json` 10mb）：按 Issue #1 thread 1 comment #46/#49，B 侧已同意不全局化、将在 B-1 文本写明“state/import 不在限额范围”；@glm-1 形式裁定前无改动。
- **A-1/A-3/A-4 待 @glm-1 裁定**（A-2 已由 B 侧 comment #30 确认）。若裁定不同，A 侧改动局部：导入端点一段、接缝一个模块、导出范围一段函数。
- 公式单元格导出原文是 **C 合入前的过渡**：`e2e/workbook-lifecycle.spec.ts` 的用例已标注“**C 合入时必须翻转为计算结果**”，整合 PR 需带翻转后的公式导出门禁。
## 5. 需要你（Issue 负责人）做的
按 comment #27 + #33 + #36 验收；确认无误后交根负责人合入 `develop`；若裁定与第 3 节的末换行判断不同，或 @glm-1 对 A-1…A-4 有不同裁定，请在原讨论说明，我在本 PR 上做局部修正并重跑三项检查。

import { CsvParseError, csvColumnCount, parseCsv, workbookNameFromFileName } from './csv.js';
 * REQ-1-3-1 / Issue #3 decision D-A5: the parser accepts everything except the
 * one invalid class the requirement names (a field opening a quote that never
 * closes). These cases are the acceptance matrix for that rule.
describe('parseCsv', () => {
  it('parses plain rows and columns in order', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('a,b,c\n1,2,3')).toEqual([
      ['a', 'b', 'c'],
      ['1', '2', '3'],
  it('preserves empty fields, including trailing ones', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('a,,c')).toEqual([['a', '', 'c']]);
    expect(parseCsv('a,b\nc,d,')).toEqual([
      ['a', 'b'],
      ['c', 'd', ''],
    expect(parseCsv(',,')).toEqual([['', '', '']]);
  it('handles commas inside double quotes', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('"a,b",c')).toEqual([['a,b', 'c']]);
  it('collapses escaped quote pairs', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('"he said ""hi""",x')).toEqual([['he said "hi"', 'x']]);
    expect(parseCsv('""""')).toEqual([['"']]);
  it('keeps line breaks inside quoted fields verbatim', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('"line1\nline2",b')).toEqual([['line1\nline2', 'b']]);
    expect(parseCsv('"line1\r\nline2",b')).toEqual([['line1\r\nline2', 'b']]);
  it('accepts UTF-8 Chinese, English and numeric text', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('地区,销售额,状态\n华东,1200,完成')).toEqual([
      ['地区', '销售额', '状态'],
      ['华东', '1200', '完成'],
  it('treats LF, CRLF and lone CR as record separators', () => {
    const expected = [
      ['c', 'd'],
    ];
    expect(parseCsv('a,b\nc,d')).toEqual(expected);
    expect(parseCsv('a,b\r\nc,d')).toEqual(expected);
    expect(parseCsv('a,b\rc,d')).toEqual(expected);
  it('does not create a record for a trailing newline or trailing blank lines', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('a,b\n')).toEqual([['a', 'b']]);
    expect(parseCsv('a,b\r\n')).toEqual([['a', 'b']]);
    expect(parseCsv('a,b\n\n')).toEqual([['a', 'b']]);
    expect(parseCsv('\n')).toEqual([]);
    expect(parseCsv('\n\n')).toEqual([]);
  it('returns no records for an empty file or a BOM-only file', () => {
    expect(parseCsv('')).toEqual([]);
    expect(parseCsv('\ufeff')).toEqual([]);

## S24:L72 2026-09-29T06:33:15.888Z message assistant
[thinking]
Wait: the min validation compares against `structure.row_count` read after the rowCount block. But if payload provides rowCount, that block already updated the DB. Good.
However, there's an issue: `minRows > structure.row_count` uses the DB structure. But when payload.rowCount is provided AND payload.minRows provided, that's consistent.
Now, if `payload.rowCount` is provided but payload.minRows is undefined and existing min_rows exceeds the new smaller rowCount → we don't touch min (no update). Should we validate that existing footprint ≤ new structure? v1.3 says the helper includes "min ≤ 所报结构" rule; the state endpoint sets structure possibly shrinking below footprint. Hmm — under B's shared helper, `min_rows/min_cols ≤ 所报结构` for both paths. If a state replay shrinks rowCount to 4 while min_rows is 6, the footprint would exceed the structure. Export logic uses max(bbox, footprint) which could then produce more rows than the structure has — not harmful (rows beyond structure are empty fields), but violates the invariant.
Should the min block also validate the *effective* min against the structure even when payload.minRows is undefined? The rule "min_rows/min_cols ≤ 所报结构" arguably applies to the resulting state. To keep the invariant true, I should validate effective mins whenever either structure or mins are touched... but that would make `PUT .../state` with rowCount=4 on an imported 6x2 worksheet fail with 400 — is that desired? Under the invariant, yes: min ≤ structure must hold. But it also could break D's undo replay of a "shrink structure" step for imported worksheets — though B's structure endpoint also must not shrink below min? Actually B's structure operations must not touch min fields (they stay static), so a structure delete could make min > structure. Hmm, that's a real hole in the contract: static footprint + shrinking structure ⇒ min > structure.
The contract says the helper rule is `min_rows/min_cols ≤ 所报结构` — applying to the values *reported*. If B deletes rows so that structure < min, B is not reading/writing min, so it can violate. That's the root's known limitation ("下限静态、不随结构操作演化"; "仅在'删除足迹内部行列后再导出'这一三阶场景下与动态演化有差异"). So min > structure is tolerated in that scenario. Then export: max(bbox, minRows) may exceed rowCount — rows beyond structure are empty. That's fine and intentional-ish.
So for state: if payload doesn't mention mins, don't validate (they keep their value, possibly exceeding a shrunken structure — same as B's structural shrink). If payload explicitly mentions a min, it must be ≤ the effective structure. That's exactly what I implemented. Good.
Now the frontend: types, csvExport.

/** Export the active worksheet and return the suggested name plus text content. */
async function exportCsv(page: Page): Promise<{ fileName: string; csv: string }> {
  const [download] = await Promise.all([
    page.waitForEvent('download'),
    page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Export CSV' }).click(),
  ]);
  const path = await download.path();
  if (!path) throw new Error('download has no local path');
  return { fileName: download.suggestedFilename(), csv: fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8') };

【根侧核查 + v1.3 增量确认：候选 d536aa2 缺 Y-min 落地项，补齐后才进入根侧验收】@deepseek-3 @deepseek-5
我已在 `origin/braid/issue-3-a` @ `d536aa2` 上直接核实（非仅依据交接文本）：
**已确认到位**：① README 三条路径窄改精确落地（第 88–89 行，同段两条不变量原文保留）；② 导入端点/单事务/结构下界/INVALID_CSV 文案；③ 末换行裁定（见下）已按建议形态实现。
**关键缺口：候选未落地契约 v1.3**。本交接写于 Issue #1 #51–#60 发布的并行期，其中「A-7 已定格（不实现导入尺寸下限）」已被 thread 1 **comment #51（A-4 Y-min 裁定）+ #54 + #57（落地细化）+ #60（时序）修订**。我在候选上核实：`min_rows`/`min_cols` 全仓无匹配；`worksheetToCsv` 为 `maxRow<0 → return ''`。按 v1.3 需补（全部归 PR #8，改动局部）：
1. **迁移**：worksheets 增可空 `min_rows`/`min_cols`；`shared/types.d.ts` 快照类型同步。
2. **导入事务**写入足迹 = CSV 实际行列数（裁剪后、含 ragged 补齐）；新建空白工作簿足迹为 0。
3. **快照输出**：`getWorksheetSnapshot` 带上两字段（state 导入返回值即它，GET 快照与 `PUT .../state` 同时生效）。
4. **state 导入**：两字段沿用 `undefined` = 不改动语义（#57 §3），缺失**不得**重置为 0。
5. **导出**：used range = max(非空包围盒, 静态足迹)；足迹下限静态、不随结构操作演化；**空表分支改为足迹优先**（#57 §4）：零字节文件仅当包围盒与足迹同时为空；足迹 > 0 时输出足迹范围内的空字段。测试注释中「零字节为契约断言」的说法相应更新。
6. **共享校验 helper**：`min ≤ 所报结构` 规则由 B 的 PR #9 承担（#54/#57 §2）；时序按 **#60**——helper 进 develop 前 PR #8 可先用私有路径，rebase 时切换为调用，`develop` 最终状态必须是调用共享 helper；该切换完成是 #3 关闭前提之一。
7. 配套：README 登记 min_rows/min_cols 语义（一段即可）；e2e/单测补「导入→清空→导出」与「导入→（无结构操作）→导出」round-trip 用例。
**末换行裁定（采纳偏离）**：`worksheetToCsv` 返回 `lines.join('\n')`、末条记录后不加终结换行——**采纳**。依据：实现方 advisor 咨询的反例分析成立（JS harness 最常见构造为 `.join('\n')`），「行尾 \n」按记录分隔符解释与 v1.3 A-4 文本相容；此裁定并入契约 v1.3（Issue #1 thread 1 comment #63），一行可逆。
@deepseek-3 验收时请以 comment #27 + #33 + #36 **+ Issue #1 契约 v1.3（#51/#54/#57/#60）**为联合判据；上表 7 项补齐并复跑三项检查后才交根侧合入。@deepseek-5 请在本 thread 给出补齐候选与增量 diff 摘要；若对 Y-min 具体语义有异议，按 #51 尾部约定在 Issue #1 thread 1 提出。

comment #65 [visible]
【契约 v1.3 增量：A-4 导出末换行裁定】@deepseek-5 @deepseek-3 @glm-4
PR #8 交接（Issue #3 thread 27 comment #62）报告一处偏离：`worksheetToCsv` 返回 `lines.join('\n')`，末条记录之后**不加**终结换行；实现方就此做过独立 advisor 咨询（反例双向对称：以 `wc -l` 或"文件以换行结束"为先验的 harness 会相反误判；JS harness 最常见期望构造是 `rows.map(r=>r.join(',')).join('\n')`）。
**裁定：采纳「无末尾终结换行」**，作为 v1.3 A-4 导出语义的一部分。理由：① #51 的「行尾 `\n`」按记录分隔符解释，与该形态相容，不构成冲突；② 需求文本对末行终止符无判据，双向失败模式对称时取实现成本与惯例（RFC 4180 末换行可选）一致的一侧；③ 一行可逆，若整合阶段取得具体 harness 证据再改。候选 `d536aa2` 已按此实现，无需改动。
其余交接内容我已在分支上核实：README 三条路径窄改精确落地；**但候选未含 v1.3 的 Y-min 落地项**（`min_rows`/`min_cols` 无匹配、空表导出仍为零字节分支）——交接写于 #51–#60 并行期，其「A-7 已定格」已被本 thread #51/#54/#57/#60 修订。补齐清单已发 Issue #3 thread 27 comment #64（迁移、导入写入足迹、快照输出、state `undefined`=不改动、导出 max(包围盒,足迹) 与足迹优先空表分支、共享 helper 切换时序、round-trip 用例），PR #8 补齐并经 @deepseek-3 验收后才进入根侧合入。
comment #66 [visible]
【共享校验 helper 已发布，@deepseek-3 可消费】（#57 §2 归 B 的 PR #9）
helper 已随 `b-issue-4-worksheets-structure` push（app 代码候选 `515597c`，三项检查通过，详见 Issue #4 交回帖）。形态定稿如下，若有改动在本 thread 说明：
- **位置/导出**：`backend/src/store.js` → `assertWorksheetStateValid(state)`（store 层单点；`validate.js` 继续保留 `assertCellWithinStructure` / `assertSelectionInBounds` 两道更窄的门）。
- **入参**（单对象；除尺寸外可选）：`{ rowCount, colCount, cells?, minRows?, minCols? }`
  - `rowCount`/`colCount`：必须正整数，否则 `INVALID_BODY`，文案与抽前逐字一致：`rowCount must be a positive integer` / `colCount must be a positive integer`。
  - `cells`：`undefined` = 本次写入不带 cells（不校验）；否则必须为数组，每项 `[row,col,raw]` 的 `row/col` 为非负整数且 < 所报结构。文案：`cells must be an array`、`Cell coordinates must be non-negative integers`、`Cell r,c is outside the worksheet structure (RxC)`。
  - `minRows`/`minCols`（Y 足迹下限）：`undefined`/`null` = 未申报、跳过；否则必须非负整数且 ≤ 所报结构。文案：`minRows must be a non-negative integer`、`minRows must not exceed the reported structure (N)`（`minCols` 对称）。**只校验不写入**：两字段的持久化、快照导出与 "`undefined` = 不改动" 语义按 #57 §3 仍归 PR #8，B 未加迁移、B 的结构端点与位移 helper 不读写它们。
- **抽取等价性**：`importWorksheetState` 已改为调用它，错误码/文案/整事务回滚与抽前一致；`backend/src/structure.test.js` 新增 6 类非法态在 helper 与 `PUT .../state` 端点的同消息断言 + 回滚断言（抽前抽后行为一致），外加 Y-min 分支与边界用例。
- **消费时序**（#58 §2）：PR #8 rebase 到含该 helper 的 develop 后，把 CSV 导入路径的尺寸/cells/足迹校验换成此调用并删掉本地副本；切换义务与跟踪在 A/root 侧。

Two key updates:
1. #65: end-of-line ruling — no trailing newline adopted; no change needed. Also confirms the 7-item Y-min list.

It's not yet in develop. Per #60 timing: PR #8 can use a private path until the helper lands in develop, then switch on rebase; develop's final state must call the shared helper.
Helper details:
- `assertWorksheetStateValid({rowCount, colCount, cells?, minRows?, minCols?})`
- minRows/minCols: `undefined`/`null` = 未申报、跳过; else non-negative int and ≤ reported structure. Messages: `minRows must be a non-negative integer`, `minRows must not exceed the reported structure (N)`.

## S24:L152 2026-09-29T06:35:51.170Z message toolResult
toolResult bash False
[text]
 * A worksheet state as it is about to be persisted: the reported structure plus
 * the cell batch and (sub-issue A's) import footprint that must fit inside it.
 * @typedef {object} WorksheetStateStructure
 * @property {number} rowCount
 * @property {number} colCount
 * @property {CellEntry[]} [cells] `undefined` = this write carries no cells
 * @property {number | null} [minRows] `undefined`/`null` = no footprint reported
 * @property {number | null} [minCols]
 * The single implementation of the invariants that decide whether a worksheet
 * state is renderable and persistable:
 * - `rowCount` / `colCount` are positive integers (the grid needs at least one
 *   row and one column, and the sparse cell table cannot express the structure);
 * - every cell of `cells` lies inside the reported structure, the same bound
 *   `PUT .../cells` enforces through `assertCellWithinStructure`;
 * - the CSV-import footprint (`minRows` / `minCols`, nullable and written by
 *   sub-issue A's import path) never exceeds the structure the same import
 *   reports — the footprint is a lower bound on the *used* area, so it can only
 *   be smaller than the structure.
 * The first two checks were extracted verbatim from `importWorksheetState`
 * (semantics unchanged) so the state endpoint and the import writer validate
 * through one code path instead of two drifting copies; the footprint branch was
 * added here as the single check point (contract v1.3, Issue #1 thread 1
 * comment #57 §2). Persisting the two footprint fields — and the "`undefined` =
 * don't change" semantics of the same write — stay with PR #8; this helper only
 * validates values when a caller reports them.
 * @param {WorksheetStateStructure} state
export function assertWorksheetStateValid(state) {
  const { rowCount, colCount, cells } = state;
  if (!Number.isInteger(rowCount) || rowCount < 1) {
    throw invalidBody('rowCount must be a positive integer');
  if (!Number.isInteger(colCount) || colCount < 1) {
    throw invalidBody('colCount must be a positive integer');
   * @param {string} field
   * @param {number | null | undefined} value
   * @param {number} bound
  const checkFootprint = (field, value, bound) => {
    if (value === undefined || value === null) return;
    if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 0) {
      throw invalidBody(`${field} must be a non-negative integer`);
    if (value > bound) {
      throw invalidBody(`${field} must not exceed the reported structure (${bound})`);
  checkFootprint('minRows', state.minRows, rowCount);
  checkFootprint('minCols', state.minCols, colCount);
  if (cells === undefined) return;
  if (!Array.isArray(cells)) throw invalidBody('cells must be an array');
  // The structure is the single fact source: a snapshot may not carry cells the
  // reported rowCount/colCount cannot show. Growing a worksheet is an explicit
  // structure operation (sub-issue B), never a side effect here.
  for (const entry of cells) {
    const [row, col] = entry;
    if (!Number.isInteger(row) || row < 0 || !Number.isInteger(col) || col < 0) {
      throw invalidBody('Cell coordinates must be non-negative integers');
    assertCellWithinStructure(row, col, rowCount, colCount);

4. `state` 两字段未申报/`null` = 不改动（缺失不重置，回放不抹足迹）
5. 导出 `max(非空包围盒, 静态足迹)` + 空表分支改为**足迹优先**
6. `min ≤ 所报结构` 校验，错误码/文案与 B 的 `assertWorksheetStateValid`（#66/#69）**逐字对齐**，helper 进 develop 后单点切换（义务记入 packet/PR 正文）
7. README 登记足迹语义；单测 + e2e 各补 round-trip 用例（含「导入→清空→导出」足迹优先）
